 - 323 -
NC: 2025:KHC:6675
WP No. 3668 of 2025
 THIS WP IS FILED UNDER ARTICLES 226 AND 227 OF
THE CONSTITUTION OF INDIA PRAYING TO- DIRECTING THE
R-2 TO 5 TO INCLUDE THE NAME OF THE PETITIONERS IN THE
ELIGIBLE VOTERS LIST PRODUCED AND PERMIT THE
PETITIONERS TO CAST THEIR VOTERS IN THE ELECTIONS TO
THE R-5 WHICH IS SCHEDULE TO BE HELD ON 09.02.2025.
 THIS PETITION, COMING ON FOR PRELIMINARY
HEARING IN ‘B’ GROUP, THIS DAY, ORDER WAS MADE
THEREIN AS UNDER:
CORAM: HON'BLE MRS JUSTICE K.S. HEMALEKHA
ORAL ORDER
The petitioners are seeking for the following
prayers:-
“1. Issue a writ of mandamus directing the
respondent no.2 to 5 include the name of the
petitioners in the eligible voters list produced
and permit the petitioners to case their votes in
the elections to the respondent No.5 which is
scheduled to be held on 9.02.2025 in the
interest of justice and equity.
2. Such other direction or order writ as may
deem ft to this Hon’ble court may kingly be
issued.”
2. This Court by way of an interim arrangement on
07.02.2025 permitted the petitioners to cast their votes in

 - 324 -
NC: 2025:KHC:6675
WP No. 3668 of 2025
the ensuing election scheduled on 09.02.2025 subject to
final outcome of this petition.
3. Heard Sri P Anand, learned counsel for the
petitioners, Sri Yogesh D Naik, learned AGA for respondent
Nos.1, 3 and 4, Sri T.L. Kiran Kumar, learned counsel for
respondent No.2 and Sri Deviprasad Shetty, learned
counsel for respondent No.5.
4. Counsel for the petitioners submits that the
election has been held as scheduled on 09.02.2025 and
the petitioners have cast their votes.
5. Reiterating various contentions raised in the
writ petition, it is mainly urged that no individual notice is
issued to the petitioners while the names of the petitioners
are excluded in the final voters list and the respondents
have acted against mandate requirement of Rule 13 (D)
(2-A) of the Karnataka Co-Operative Societies Rules, 1960
(‘the Rules’ for short). It is urged that the petitioners

 - 325 -
NC: 2025:KHC:6675
WP No. 3668 of 2025
should be treated as eligible voters and their votes to be
counted as valid votes.
6. Learned counsel for respondent No.5 - Society
fairly submits that the society has not followed the
mandatory requirement of the aforementioned rules and
cast votes of the petitioners to be included while counting
and the results to be announced.
7. Respondent No.5 – Society has not placed any
material to indicate the compliance of aforementioned
rules. It is brought to the notice of this Court that under
identical circumstances, the Co-Ordinate Bench of this
Court in the case of H.S. Raju and others Vs. State of
Karnataka and others1 (H.S. Raju) has permitted the
votes to be counted and election results to be
announced, keeping open all contentions to be
adjudicated in the appropriate forum under Section 70
(2) (c) of the Karnataka Co-Operative Societies Act, 1959

1 2022 (4) AKR 775

 - 326 -
NC: 2025:KHC:6675
WP No. 3668 of 2025
(‘the Act’ for short). Whether the petitioners would incur
any disqualification to vote or not is a question of fact to
be decided by the appropriate authority under Section 70
(2) (c) of the Act and hence, the following:
ORDER
i. Writ petition is disposed of.
ii. The Returning Officer to count the votes,
including the votes cast by petitioners and
announce the results of the election, which
was scheduled on 09.02.2025.
iii. Aggrieved party is entitled to raise a
dispute under Section 70 (2) (c) of the Act,
if raised a dispute under the said provision,
questioning the eligibility of the petitioners
to vote in the election to the Board of the
respondent – Society, the said dispute shall
be decided without being influenced by the
interim order granted by this Court and it is
made clear that granting of an interim
order in favour of the petitioners would not
give any finding on the eligibility of the

 - 327 -
NC: 2025:KHC:6675
WP No. 3668 of 2025
petitioners to vote in the election of the
Board of respondent – Society.
iv. The eligibility of the petitioners to vote in
the election is kept open to be decided in a
dispute, if any, raised in this regard.
v. If any such dispute relating to the eligibility
of the petitioners to vote in the election to
the Board of the respondent - society is
raised, the effect of holding the petitioners
ineligible to vote and excluding such votes,
on the final outcome of the election results
shall also be taken into account before
passing the final orders.
vi. The vote cast by the petitioners shall be
kept in a separate sealed box after
counting.
vii. This Court has not expressed any merits or
demerits of the ineligibility of the
petitioners.
 Sd/-
____________________
 JUSTICE K.S. HEMALEKHA
